#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

mkdir -p app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/data
mkdir -p app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/ui/theme
mkdir -p app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/ui/home
mkdir -p app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/ui/player
mkdir -p app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/engine
mkdir -p app/src/main/res/drawable
mkdir -p app/src/main/res/layout
mkdir -p app/src/main/res/values
mkdir -p app/src/main/res/xml

mkdir -p app/src/main/res/font

if ! command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1 || ! command -v file >/dev/null 2>&1 || [ ! -f /usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf ]; then
    sudo apt-get update -y && sudo apt-get install -y fonts-dejavu-core curl file
fi

download_or_fallback_font() {
    local url="$1"
    local dest="$2"
    local fallback="$3"
    curl -sL --retry 3 --connect-timeout 10 "$url" -o "$dest" || true
    if [ ! -s "$dest" ] || ! file "$dest" | grep -qiE "font|TrueType|OpenType"; then
        cp "$fallback" "$dest"
    fi
}

download_or_fallback_font \
    "https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/plusjakartasans/PlusJakartaSans%5Bwght%5D.ttf" \
    "app/src/main/res/font/plus_jakarta_sans.ttf" \
    "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf"

download_or_fallback_font \
    "https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/opensans/OpenSans%5Bwdth,wght%5D.ttf" \
    "app/src/main/res/font/open_sans.ttf" \
    "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf"

cat << 'EOF' > settings.gradle.kts
pluginManagement {
    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
        gradlePluginPortal()
    }
}

    repositoriesMode.set(RepositoriesMode.FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS)
    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
    }
}
rootProject.name = "VDXPlayer"
include(":app")
EOF

enum class AppThemeMode(val label: String) {
    OCEAN("Ocean"),
    FOREST_EMERALD("Emerald forest"),
    ROYAL_VIOLET("Royal violet"),
    BANANA("Banana"),
    TERRACOTTA("Terracotta"),

}

    BLUE("Ocean blue", "com.flagship.videoplayer.LauncherBlue", 0xFF4D9CFF),
    EMERALD("Emerald green", "com.flagship.videoplayer.LauncherEmerald", 0xFF10B981),

}

    DECODER("DECODER", "Decoder selector"),
    PIP("PIP", "Picture-in-Picture"),
    STATS("STATS", "Codec & renderer stats")
}

data class VideoTechnicalMetadata(
    val videoCodec: String,
    val audioCodec: String,
    val bitrateFormatted: String,
    val frameRateFormatted: String,
    val hdrFormatDetails: String,
    val colorSpaceDetails: String,
    val isHdr: Boolean
)

object VideoTechnicalProber {
    suspend fun probeVideoFile(context: Context, video: LocalVideoItem): VideoTechnicalMetadata =
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            var vCodec = video.extensionBadge
            var aCodec = "None / Unknown"
            var bitrateBps = 0L
            var fps = 0f
            var colorTransfer = 0
            var colorStandard = 0
            var profile = 0

            runCatching {
                val extractor = MediaExtractor()
                try {
                    if (File(video.filePath).canRead()) {
                        extractor.setDataSource(video.filePath)
                    } else {
                        extractor.setDataSource(context, video.contentUri, null)
                    }
                    for (i in 0 until extractor.trackCount) {
                        val fmt = extractor.getTrackFormat(i)
                        val mime = fmt.getString(MediaFormat.KEY_MIME) ?: ""
                        if (mime.startsWith("video/")) {
                            vCodec = formatMimeToCodecName(mime)
                            if (fmt.containsKey(MediaFormat.KEY_BIT_RATE)) {
                                bitrateBps = fmt.getInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_BIT_RATE).toLong()
                            }
                            if (fmt.containsKey(MediaFormat.KEY_FRAME_RATE)) {
                                fps = fmt.getInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_FRAME_RATE).toFloat()
                            }
                            if (fmt.containsKey(MediaFormat.KEY_COLOR_TRANSFER)) {
                                colorTransfer = fmt.getInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_COLOR_TRANSFER)
                            }
                            if (fmt.containsKey(MediaFormat.KEY_COLOR_STANDARD)) {
                                colorStandard = fmt.getInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_COLOR_STANDARD)
                            }
                            if (fmt.containsKey(MediaFormat.KEY_PROFILE)) {
                                profile = fmt.getInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_PROFILE)
                            }
                        } else if (mime.startsWith("audio/")) {
                            val ch = if (fmt.containsKey(MediaFormat.KEY_CHANNEL_COUNT)) {
                                fmt.getInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_CHANNEL_COUNT)
                            } else 2
                            aCodec = "${formatMimeToCodecName(mime)} (${ch}ch)"
                        }
                    }
                } finally {
                    extractor.release()
                }
            }

            if (bitrateBps <= 0L) {
                runCatching {
                    val retriever = MediaMetadataRetriever()
                    try {
                        if (File(video.filePath).canRead()) {
                            retriever.setDataSource(video.filePath)
                        } else {
                            retriever.setDataSource(context, video.contentUri)
                        }
                        retriever.extractMetadata(MediaMetadataRetriever.METADATA_KEY_BITRATE)?.toLongOrNull()?.let {
                            bitrateBps = it
                        }
                    } finally {
                        retriever.release()
                    }
                }
            }

            if (bitrateBps <= 0L && video.durationMs > 0L && video.sizeBytes > 0L) {
                bitrateBps = (video.sizeBytes * 8L * 1000L) / video.durationMs
            }

            val bitrateStr = when {
                bitrateBps >= 1_000_000L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f Mbps", bitrateBps / 1_000_000.0)
                bitrateBps >= 1_000L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%d kbps", bitrateBps / 1_000L)
                else -> "Variable"
            }

            val fpsStr = if (fps > 0f) String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f fps", fps) else "Standard"

            val isDolbyVision = vCodec.contains("Dolby Vision", ignoreCase = true)
            val isPqHdr10 = colorTransfer == MediaFormat.COLOR_TRANSFER_ST2084
            val isHlg = colorTransfer == MediaFormat.COLOR_TRANSFER_HLG
            val isBt2020 = colorStandard == MediaFormat.COLOR_STANDARD_BT2020
            val isHdr = isDolbyVision || isPqHdr10 || isHlg || isBt2020

            val hdrDetails = when {
                isDolbyVision -> "Dolby Vision (10-bit Dynamic HDR)"
                isPqHdr10 && profile >= 4096 -> "HDR10+ / PQ (SMPTE ST 2084 • 10-bit)"
                isPqHdr10 -> "HDR10 (SMPTE ST 2084 PQ • 10-bit)"
                isHlg -> "HLG (Hybrid Log-Gamma HDR • 10-bit)"
                isBt2020 -> "Wide Gamut BT.2020 HDR"
                else -> "SDR (Standard Dynamic Range • Rec.709 8-bit)"
            }

            val colorSpaceStr = when (colorStandard) {
                MediaFormat.COLOR_STANDARD_BT2020 -> "BT.2020 Wide Gamut"
                MediaFormat.COLOR_STANDARD_BT709 -> "Rec.709 Standard"
                else -> if (isHdr) "BT.2020" else "Rec.709"
            }

            VideoTechnicalMetadata(
                videoCodec = vCodec,
                audioCodec = aCodec,
                bitrateFormatted = bitrateStr,
                frameRateFormatted = fpsStr,
                hdrFormatDetails = hdrDetails,
                colorSpaceDetails = colorSpaceStr,
                isHdr = isHdr
            )
        }

    private fun formatMimeToCodecName(mime: String): String {
        val lower = mime.lowercase()
        return when {
            lower.contains("hevc") || lower.contains("h265") -> "HEVC / H.265"
            lower.contains("avc") || lower.contains("h264") -> "AVC / H.264"
            lower.contains("dolby-vision") || lower.contains("dvhe") -> "Dolby Vision HEVC"
            lower.contains("av01") || lower.contains("av1") -> "AV1"
            lower.contains("vp9") -> "VP9"
            lower.contains("vp8") -> "VP8"
            lower.contains("mp4a") || lower.contains("aac") -> "AAC"
            lower.contains("ac3") -> "Dolby AC-3"
            lower.contains("eac3") -> "Dolby Digital Plus (E-AC3)"
            lower.contains("flac") -> "FLAC Lossless"
            lower.contains("opus") -> "Opus"
            lower.contains("vorbis") -> "Vorbis"
            lower.contains("dts") -> "DTS Coherent Acoustics"
            else -> mime.substringAfter('/').uppercase()
        }
    }
}

object AppLauncherIconManager {
    fun applyLauncherIcon(context: Context, selectedColor: AppIconColor) {
        val pm = context.packageManager
        AppIconColor.entries.forEach { option ->
            val component = ComponentName(context.packageName, option.aliasName)
            val newState = if (option == selectedColor) {
                PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_ENABLED
            } else {
                PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_DISABLED
            }
            runCatching {
                pm.setComponentEnabledSetting(
                    component,
                    newState,
                    PackageManager.DONT_KILL_APP
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

data class LocalVideoItem(
    val mediaStoreId: Long,
    val contentUri: Uri,
    val filePath: String,
    val folderPath: String,
    val folderName: String,
    val displayName: String,
    val titleWithoutExt: String,
    val extensionBadge: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val width: Int,
    val height: Int,
    val resolutionBadge: String,
    val dateModifiedSec: Long,
    val sidecarSubtitlePaths: List<String>,
    val fileFingerprint: String,
    val savedPositionMs: Long = 0L,
    val lastPlayedAtMs: Long = 0L,
    val isNetworkStream: Boolean = false,
    val customHeaders: Map<String, String> = emptyMap(),
    val userAgent: String = "",
    val referer: String = ""
) {
    val completionFraction: Float
        get() = if (durationMs > 0L) (savedPositionMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f) else 0f

    val hasSidecarSubtitles: Boolean
        get() = sidecarSubtitlePaths.isNotEmpty()
}

data class VideoFolderItem(
    val folderPath: String,
    val folderName: String,
    val videos: List<LocalVideoItem>,
    val totalSizeBytes: Long,
    val unplayedCount: Int,
    val lastPlayedVideo: LocalVideoItem?
)

@Entity(tableName = "cached_library_videos")
data class CachedVideoEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val filePath: String,
    val mediaStoreId: Long,
    val contentUriString: String,
    val folderPath: String,
    val folderName: String,
    val displayName: String,
    val titleWithoutExt: String,
    val extensionBadge: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val width: Int,
    val height: Int,
    val resolutionBadge: String,
    val dateModifiedSec: Long,
    val sidecarPathsJoined: String,
    val fileFingerprint: String
) {
    fun toLocalVideoItem(savedState: VideoStateEntity?): LocalVideoItem {
        val sidecars = if (sidecarPathsJoined.isBlank()) emptyList() else sidecarPathsJoined.split("|")
        return LocalVideoItem(
            mediaStoreId = mediaStoreId,
            contentUri = Uri.parse(contentUriString),
            filePath = filePath,
            folderPath = folderPath,
            folderName = folderName,
            displayName = displayName,
            titleWithoutExt = titleWithoutExt,
            extensionBadge = extensionBadge,
            sizeBytes = sizeBytes,
            durationMs = if (durationMs > 0L) durationMs else (savedState?.durationMs ?: 0L),
            width = width,
            height = height,
            resolutionBadge = resolutionBadge,
            dateModifiedSec = dateModifiedSec,
            sidecarSubtitlePaths = sidecars,
            fileFingerprint = fileFingerprint,
            savedPositionMs = savedState?.positionMs ?: 0L,
            lastPlayedAtMs = savedState?.lastPlayedAtMs ?: 0L
        )
    }
}

@Entity(tableName = "video_states")
data class VideoStateEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val fileFingerprint: String,
    val filePath: String,
    val folderPath: String,
    val displayName: String,
    val positionMs: Long = 0L,
    val durationMs: Long = 0L,
    val lastPlayedAtMs: Long = 0L,
    val playbackSpeed: Float = 1.0f,
    val decoderMode: String = DecoderMode.HW_PLUS.name,
    val forceSdrToneMapping: Boolean = false,
    val zoomScale: Float = 1.0f,
    val panOffsetX: Float = 0f,
    val panOffsetY: Float = 0f,
    val aspectRatioMode: String = AspectRatioMode.FIT.name,
    val orientationMode: String = PlayerOrientationMode.SENSOR_LANDSCAPE.name,
    val audioTrackIndex: Int = -1,
    val isAudioDisabled: Boolean = false,
    val externalAudioUri: String? = null,
    val audioDelayMs: Long = 0L,
    val subtitleTrackIndex: Int = -1,
    val subtitlesEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val externalSubtitleUri: String? = null,
    val subtitleDelayMs: Long = 0L,
    val subtitleSpeedFactor: Float = 1.0f,
    val subtitleAnchorTimeMs: Long = 0L,
    val activeLoopStartMs: Long? = null,
    val activeLoopEndMs: Long? = null
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "video_bookmarks",
    indices = [Index(value = ["videoFingerprint"])]
)
data class BookmarkEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0L,
    val videoFingerprint: String,
    val timestampMs: Long,
    val label: String,
    val createdAtMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "saved_ab_loops",
    indices = [Index(value = ["videoFingerprint"])]
)
data class SavedLoopEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0L,
    val videoFingerprint: String,
    val label: String,
    val startMs: Long,
    val endMs: Long,
    val createdAtMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

@Entity(tableName = "trashed_videos")
data class TrashedVideoEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0L,
    val fileFingerprint: String,
    val originalPath: String,
    val trashPath: String,
    val displayName: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val trashedAtMs: Long,
    val expiresAtMs: Long
) {
    fun daysRemaining(nowMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Int {
        val diff = (expiresAtMs - nowMs).coerceAtLeast(0L)
        return ((diff / (24L * 3600_000L)) + 1L).toInt().coerceAtMost(30)
    }
}

@Entity(tableName = "network_stream_history")
data class NetworkStreamHistoryEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val streamUrl: String,
    val title: String,
    val protocolBadge: String,
    val userAgent: String = "",
    val referer: String = "",
    val headersSerialized: String = "",
    val lastPositionMs: Long = 0L,
    val durationMs: Long = 0L,
    val lastPlayedAtMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
) {
    fun parsedHeaders(): Map<String, String> = parseRawHeaders(headersSerialized)

    companion object {
        fun parseRawHeaders(raw: String): Map<String, String> {
            if (raw.isBlank()) return emptyMap()
            return raw.lines()
                .mapNotNull { line ->
                    val idx = line.indexOf(':')
                    if (idx > 0) line.substring(0, idx).trim() to line.substring(idx + 1).trim()
                    else null
                }
                .filter { it.first.isNotEmpty() }
                .toMap()
        }

        fun serializeHeaders(headers: Map<String, String>): String {
            return headers.entries.joinToString("\n") { "${it.key}: ${it.value}" }
        }

        fun detectProtocolBadge(url: String): String {
            val lower = url.lowercase()
            return when {
                lower.startsWith("rtsp://") -> "RTSP"
                lower.contains(".m3u8") -> "HLS"
                lower.contains(".mpd") -> "DASH"
                lower.contains(".ism") -> "SMOOTH"
                lower.contains(".mkv") -> "MKV"
                lower.contains(".webm") -> "WEBM"
                lower.startsWith("https://") -> "HTTPS"
                else -> "HTTP"
            }
        }
    }
}

@Dao
interface FlagshipPlayerDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM cached_library_videos")
    fun observeCachedVideos(): Flow<List<CachedVideoEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM cached_library_videos")
    suspend fun getCachedVideosOnce(): List<CachedVideoEntity>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun upsertCachedVideos(videos: List<CachedVideoEntity>)

    @Query("DELETE FROM cached_library_videos WHERE filePath IN (:paths)")
    suspend fun deleteCachedVideosByPath(paths: List<String>)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM video_states")
    fun observeAllVideoStates(): Flow<List<VideoStateEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM video_states WHERE fileFingerprint = :fingerprint LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getVideoState(fingerprint: String): VideoStateEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun upsertVideoState(state: VideoStateEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM video_states WHERE fileFingerprint = :fingerprint")
    suspend fun deleteVideoStateByFingerprint(fingerprint: String)

    @Query("UPDATE video_states SET fileFingerprint = :newFp, filePath = :newPath, folderPath = :newFolder, displayName = :newDisplayName WHERE fileFingerprint = :oldFp")
    suspend fun migrateVideoStateFingerprint(
        oldFp: String,
        newFp: String,
        newPath: String,
        newFolder: String,
        newDisplayName: String
    )

    @Query("UPDATE video_states SET filePath = :newPath, folderPath = :newFolder, displayName = :newDisplayName WHERE fileFingerprint = :fingerprint")
    suspend fun updateRenamedVideoMetadata(
        fingerprint: String,
        newPath: String,
        newFolder: String,
        newDisplayName: String
    )

    @Query("UPDATE video_bookmarks SET videoFingerprint = :newFp WHERE videoFingerprint = :oldFp")
    suspend fun migrateBookmarksFingerprint(oldFp: String, newFp: String)

    @Query("UPDATE saved_ab_loops SET videoFingerprint = :newFp WHERE videoFingerprint = :oldFp")
    suspend fun migrateSavedLoopsFingerprint(oldFp: String, newFp: String)

    @Transaction
    suspend fun migrateRenamedVideoRecords(
        oldFp: String,
        newFp: String,
        newPath: String,
        newFolder: String,
        newDisplayName: String
    ) {
        if (oldFp != newFp) {
            deleteVideoStateByFingerprint(newFp)
            migrateVideoStateFingerprint(oldFp, newFp, newPath, newFolder, newDisplayName)
            migrateBookmarksFingerprint(oldFp, newFp)
            migrateSavedLoopsFingerprint(oldFp, newFp)
        } else {
            updateRenamedVideoMetadata(oldFp, newPath, newFolder, newDisplayName)
        }
    }

    @Query("SELECT * FROM video_bookmarks WHERE videoFingerprint = :fingerprint ORDER BY timestampMs ASC")
    fun observeBookmarks(fingerprint: String): Flow<List<BookmarkEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertBookmark(bookmark: BookmarkEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateBookmark(bookmark: BookmarkEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteBookmark(bookmark: BookmarkEntity)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM saved_ab_loops WHERE videoFingerprint = :fingerprint ORDER BY startMs ASC")
    fun observeSavedLoops(fingerprint: String): Flow<List<SavedLoopEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSavedLoop(loop: SavedLoopEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateSavedLoop(loop: SavedLoopEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteSavedLoop(loop: SavedLoopEntity)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM trashed_videos ORDER BY trashedAtMs DESC")
    fun observeTrashedVideos(): Flow<List<TrashedVideoEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM trashed_videos")
    suspend fun getAllTrashedVideosOnce(): List<TrashedVideoEntity>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertTrashedVideo(item: TrashedVideoEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteTrashedVideoRecord(item: TrashedVideoEntity)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM trashed_videos WHERE expiresAtMs < :nowMs")
    suspend fun getExpiredTrashedVideos(nowMs: Long): List<TrashedVideoEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM network_stream_history ORDER BY lastPlayedAtMs DESC")
    fun observeNetworkStreams(): Flow<List<NetworkStreamHistoryEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun upsertNetworkStream(stream: NetworkStreamHistoryEntity)

    @Query("UPDATE network_stream_history SET title = :newTitle WHERE streamUrl = :streamUrl")
    suspend fun renameNetworkStream(streamUrl: String, newTitle: String)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteNetworkStream(stream: NetworkStreamHistoryEntity)
}

@Database(
    entities = [
        CachedVideoEntity::class,
        VideoStateEntity::class,
        BookmarkEntity::class,
        SavedLoopEntity::class,
        TrashedVideoEntity::class,
        NetworkStreamHistoryEntity::class
    ],
    version = 7,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class FlagshipDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun dao(): FlagshipPlayerDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: FlagshipDatabase? = null

        private val MIGRATION_6_7 = object : Migration(6, 7) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE video_states ADD COLUMN activeLoopStartMs INTEGER")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE video_states ADD COLUMN activeLoopEndMs INTEGER")
            }
        }

        fun getInstance(context: Context): FlagshipDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    FlagshipDatabase::class.java,
                    "vdx_flagship_player_v6.db"
                )
                    .addMigrations(MIGRATION_6_7)
                    .fallbackToDestructiveMigrationOnDowngrade()
                    .build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }
    }
}

object FileFingerprintEngine {
    private val cache = java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<String, String>()

    fun computeQuickFingerprint(fileName: String, sizeBytes: Long): String {
        val key = "$fileName:$sizeBytes"
        return cache.getOrPut(key) {
            sha256String("vdx_fp:$fileName:$sizeBytes")
        }
    }

    fun computeFingerprint(filePath: String, sizeBytes: Long): String {
        val file = File(filePath)
        return computeQuickFingerprint(file.name, sizeBytes)
    }

    fun computeStreamFingerprint(url: String): String {
        return "stream_" + sha256String(url.trim())
    }

    private fun sha256String(input: String): String {
        val bytes = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(input.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
        return bytes.joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
    }
}

sealed class MediaOperationOutcome {
    data class Completed(val message: String) : MediaOperationOutcome()
    data class NeedsSystemConsent(val intentSender: IntentSender) : MediaOperationOutcome()
    data class Failed(val reason: String) : MediaOperationOutcome()
}

object TrashAndRenameManager {
    private const val THIRTY_DAYS_MS = 30L * 24L * 3600L * 1000L

    private fun getTrashVaultDir(context: Context): File {
        val baseDir = context.getExternalFilesDir(null) ?: context.filesDir
        val vault = File(baseDir, ".trash_vault")
        if (!vault.exists()) vault.mkdirs()
        return vault
    }

    suspend fun moveVideoToTrash(
        context: Context,
        dao: FlagshipPlayerDao,
        video: LocalVideoItem
    ): MediaOperationOutcome = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val sourceFile = File(video.filePath)
        val vaultDir = getTrashVaultDir(context)
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val safeTrashName = "${now}_${sourceFile.name}"
        val destFile = File(vaultDir, safeTrashName)

        // Try direct vault move first (works on Android 8-10 and app-owned/writable directories)
        var movedToVault = false
        if (sourceFile.exists() && sourceFile.canWrite()) {
            movedToVault = runCatching { sourceFile.renameTo(destFile) }.getOrDefault(false)
            if (!movedToVault) {
                movedToVault = runCatching {
                    FileInputStream(sourceFile).use { input ->
                        FileOutputStream(destFile).use { output ->
                            input.copyTo(output, bufferSize = 256 * 1024)
                        }
                    }
                    val copiedOk = destFile.exists() && destFile.length() == sourceFile.length()
                    if (copiedOk && sourceFile.delete()) {
                        true
                    } else {
                        destFile.delete()
                        false
                    }
                }.getOrDefault(false)
            }
        }

        if (movedToVault) {
            runCatching { context.contentResolver.delete(video.contentUri, null, null) }
            val entity = TrashedVideoEntity(
                fileFingerprint = video.fileFingerprint,
                originalPath = video.filePath,
                trashPath = destFile.absolutePath,
                displayName = video.displayName,
                sizeBytes = video.sizeBytes,
                durationMs = video.durationMs,
                trashedAtMs = now,
                expiresAtMs = now + THIRTY_DAYS_MS
            )
            dao.insertTrashedVideo(entity)
            dao.deleteCachedVideosByPath(listOf(video.filePath))
            return@withContext MediaOperationOutcome.Completed("Moved \"${video.displayName}\" to Recycle bin")
        }

        // Android 11+ Scoped Storage: request native MediaStore Trash consent dialog
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R && video.mediaStoreId >= 0L) {
            val pi = runCatching {
                MediaStore.createTrashRequest(context.contentResolver, listOf(video.contentUri), true)
            }.getOrNull()
            if (pi != null) {
                return@withContext MediaOperationOutcome.NeedsSystemConsent(pi.intentSender)
            }
        }

        MediaOperationOutcome.Failed("Android blocked direct file move. Permission denied.")
    }

    suspend fun finalizeSystemTrash(
        dao: FlagshipPlayerDao,
        video: LocalVideoItem
    ) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        dao.deleteCachedVideosByPath(listOf(video.filePath))
    }

    suspend fun restoreFromTrash(
        context: Context,
        dao: FlagshipPlayerDao,
        trashed: TrashedVideoEntity
    ): Result<Boolean> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        runCatching {
            val trashFile = File(trashed.trashPath)
            val originalFile = File(trashed.originalPath)
            originalFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()

            require(trashFile.exists()) { "File no longer exists in Recycle bin" }

            if (trashFile.absolutePath != originalFile.absolutePath) {
                val renamed = trashFile.renameTo(originalFile)
                if (!renamed) {
                    FileInputStream(trashFile).use { input ->
                        FileOutputStream(originalFile).use { output ->
                            input.copyTo(output, bufferSize = 256 * 1024)
                        }
                    }
                    require(originalFile.exists() && originalFile.length() == trashFile.length()) {
                        "Could not restore file to original folder"
                    }
                    trashFile.delete()
                }
            }
            android.media.MediaScannerConnection.scanFile(
                context,
                arrayOf(originalFile.absolutePath),
                null,
                null
            )
            dao.deleteTrashedVideoRecord(trashed)
            true
        }
    }

    suspend fun purgeSingleTrashedVideo(
        dao: FlagshipPlayerDao,
        trashed: TrashedVideoEntity
    ) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        runCatching {
            val trashFile = File(trashed.trashPath)
            if (trashFile.exists()) trashFile.delete()
        }
        dao.deleteTrashedVideoRecord(trashed)
    }

    suspend fun purgeExpiredTrash(dao: FlagshipPlayerDao) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val expired = dao.getExpiredTrashedVideos(now)
        for (item in expired) {
            purgeSingleTrashedVideo(dao, item)
        }
    }

    suspend fun renameVideoPreservingState(
        context: Context,
        dao: FlagshipPlayerDao,
        video: LocalVideoItem,
        newNameWithoutExt: String,
        alreadyConsented: Boolean = false
    ): MediaOperationOutcome = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val cleanBase = newNameWithoutExt.trim().replace(Regex("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|]"), "_")
        if (cleanBase.isEmpty()) {
            return@withContext MediaOperationOutcome.Failed("File name cannot be empty")
        }

        val oldFile = File(video.filePath)
        val ext = oldFile.extension.ifEmpty { video.extensionBadge.lowercase() }
        val newDisplayName = if (ext.isNotEmpty()) "$cleanBase.$ext" else cleanBase
        val parentDir = oldFile.parentFile ?: File(video.folderPath)
        val newFile = File(parentDir, newDisplayName)

        if (newDisplayName == video.displayName) {
            return@withContext MediaOperationOutcome.Completed("Name unchanged")
        }

        var success = false
        if (!alreadyConsented && oldFile.exists() && oldFile.canWrite() && !newFile.exists()) {
            success = runCatching { oldFile.renameTo(newFile) }.getOrDefault(false)
        }

        if (!success) {
            val values = ContentValues().apply {
                put(MediaStore.Video.Media.DISPLAY_NAME, newDisplayName)
            }
            val updatedRows = runCatching {
                context.contentResolver.update(video.contentUri, values, null, null)
            }.getOrDefault(0)
            success = updatedRows > 0
        }

        if (!success && !alreadyConsented && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R && video.mediaStoreId >= 0L) {
            val pi = runCatching {
                MediaStore.createWriteRequest(context.contentResolver, listOf(video.contentUri))
            }.getOrNull()
            if (pi != null) {
                return@withContext MediaOperationOutcome.NeedsSystemConsent(pi.intentSender)
            }
        }

        if (!success) {
            return@withContext MediaOperationOutcome.Failed("Rename failed: permission denied or file already exists")
        }

        val newFingerprint = FileFingerprintEngine.computeQuickFingerprint(newDisplayName, video.sizeBytes)
        dao.migrateRenamedVideoRecords(
            oldFp = video.fileFingerprint,
            newFp = newFingerprint,
            newPath = newFile.absolutePath,
            newFolder = parentDir.absolutePath,
            newDisplayName = newDisplayName
        )
        dao.deleteCachedVideosByPath(listOf(video.filePath))
        android.media.MediaScannerConnection.scanFile(
            context,
            arrayOf(oldFile.absolutePath, newFile.absolutePath),
            null,
            null
        )
        MediaOperationOutcome.Completed("Renamed to $newDisplayName")
    }
}

private val Context.dataStore: DataStore<Preferences> by preferencesDataStore(name = "vdx_player_prefs_v6")

data class AppPreferencesState(
    val themeMode: AppThemeMode = AppThemeMode.OCEAN,
    val appIconColor: AppIconColor = AppIconColor.BLUE,
    val libraryViewMode: LibraryViewMode = LibraryViewMode.RICH_LIST,
    val folderContentViewMode: FolderContentViewMode = FolderContentViewMode.RICH_LIST,
    val sortField: LibrarySortField = LibrarySortField.DATE_MODIFIED,
    val sortAscending: Boolean = false,
    val defaultDecoderMode: DecoderMode = DecoderMode.HW_PLUS,
    val holdFastForwardSpeed: Float = 2.0f,
    val skipIntervalSeconds: Int = 10,
    val autoPipEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val backgroundAudioEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val topLeftSlotsCsv: String = "SPEED,BOOKMARK,LOOP,ORIENTATION",
    val topRightSlotsCsv: String = "LOCK,DECODER",
    val subtitleFont: SubtitleFontChoice = SubtitleFontChoice.SANS_SERIF,
    val subtitleFontSizeSp: Float = 22f,
    val subtitleTextColorArgb: Int = 0xFFFFFFFF.toInt(),
    val subtitleBgOpacity: Float = 0.25f,
    val subtitleDropShadowStrength: Float = 0.70f,
    val subtitleOutlineWidth: Float = 0.55f,
    val subtitleBold: Boolean = true,
    val subtitleBottomMarginDp: Float = 28f
) {
    val enabledTopLeftSlots: List<TopLeftQuickSlot>
        get() = topLeftSlotsCsv.split(",")
            .mapNotNull { token -> TopLeftQuickSlot.entries.find { it.key == token.trim() } }

    val enabledTopRightSlots: List<TopRightQuickSlot>
        get() = topRightSlotsCsv.split(",")
            .mapNotNull { token -> TopRightQuickSlot.entries.find { it.key == token.trim() } }
}

class AppPreferencesRepository(private val context: Context) {
    private object Keys {
        val THEME_MODE = stringPreferencesKey("theme_mode_v6")
        val APP_ICON_COLOR = stringPreferencesKey("app_icon_color_v6")
        val VIEW_MODE = stringPreferencesKey("library_view_mode")
        val FOLDER_CONTENT_VIEW_MODE = stringPreferencesKey("folder_content_view_mode")
        val SORT_FIELD = stringPreferencesKey("sort_field")
        val SORT_ASC = booleanPreferencesKey("sort_ascending")
        val DEFAULT_DECODER = stringPreferencesKey("default_decoder")
        val HOLD_FF_SPEED = floatPreferencesKey("hold_ff_speed")
        val SKIP_INTERVAL_SEC = intPreferencesKey("skip_interval_sec")
        val AUTO_PIP = booleanPreferencesKey("auto_pip")
        val BG_AUDIO = booleanPreferencesKey("bg_audio")
        val TOP_LEFT_SLOTS = stringPreferencesKey("top_left_slots_csv")
        val TOP_RIGHT_SLOTS = stringPreferencesKey("top_right_slots_csv")
        val SUB_FONT = stringPreferencesKey("sub_font_v6")
        val SUB_SIZE_SP = floatPreferencesKey("sub_size_sp")
        val SUB_COLOR = intPreferencesKey("sub_color_argb_v6")
        val SUB_BG_OPACITY = floatPreferencesKey("sub_bg_opacity")
        val SUB_SHADOW_STRENGTH = floatPreferencesKey("sub_shadow_strength_v6")
        val SUB_OUTLINE_WIDTH = floatPreferencesKey("sub_outline_width_v6")
        val SUB_BOLD = booleanPreferencesKey("sub_bold")
        val SUB_BOTTOM_MARGIN = floatPreferencesKey("sub_bottom_margin")
    }

    val preferencesFlow: Flow<AppPreferencesState> = context.dataStore.data
        .catch { emit(emptyPreferences()) }
        .map { prefs ->
            AppPreferencesState(
                themeMode = runCatching {
                    AppThemeMode.valueOf(prefs[Keys.THEME_MODE] ?: AppThemeMode.OCEAN.name)
                }.getOrDefault(AppThemeMode.OCEAN),
                appIconColor = runCatching {
                    AppIconColor.valueOf(prefs[Keys.APP_ICON_COLOR] ?: AppIconColor.BLUE.name)
                }.getOrDefault(AppIconColor.BLUE),
                libraryViewMode = runCatching {
                    LibraryViewMode.valueOf(prefs[Keys.VIEW_MODE] ?: LibraryViewMode.RICH_LIST.name)
                }.getOrDefault(LibraryViewMode.RICH_LIST),
                folderContentViewMode = runCatching {
                    FolderContentViewMode.valueOf(prefs[Keys.FOLDER_CONTENT_VIEW_MODE] ?: FolderContentViewMode.RICH_LIST.name)
                }.getOrDefault(FolderContentViewMode.RICH_LIST),
                sortField = runCatching {
                    LibrarySortField.valueOf(prefs[Keys.SORT_FIELD] ?: LibrarySortField.DATE_MODIFIED.name)
                }.getOrDefault(LibrarySortField.DATE_MODIFIED),
                sortAscending = prefs[Keys.SORT_ASC] ?: false,
                defaultDecoderMode = runCatching {
                    DecoderMode.valueOf(prefs[Keys.DEFAULT_DECODER] ?: DecoderMode.HW_PLUS.name)
                }.getOrDefault(DecoderMode.HW_PLUS),
                holdFastForwardSpeed = (prefs[Keys.HOLD_FF_SPEED] ?: 2.0f).coerceIn(1.25f, 3.5f),
                skipIntervalSeconds = (prefs[Keys.SKIP_INTERVAL_SEC] ?: 10).coerceIn(3, 30),
                autoPipEnabled = prefs[Keys.AUTO_PIP] ?: true,
                backgroundAudioEnabled = prefs[Keys.BG_AUDIO] ?: false,
                topLeftSlotsCsv = prefs[Keys.TOP_LEFT_SLOTS] ?: "SPEED,BOOKMARK,LOOP,ORIENTATION",
                topRightSlotsCsv = prefs[Keys.TOP_RIGHT_SLOTS] ?: "LOCK,DECODER",
                subtitleFont = runCatching {
                    SubtitleFontChoice.valueOf(prefs[Keys.SUB_FONT] ?: SubtitleFontChoice.SANS_SERIF.name)
                }.getOrDefault(SubtitleFontChoice.SANS_SERIF),
                subtitleFontSizeSp = prefs[Keys.SUB_SIZE_SP] ?: 22f,
                subtitleTextColorArgb = prefs[Keys.SUB_COLOR] ?: 0xFFFFFFFF.toInt(),
                subtitleBgOpacity = prefs[Keys.SUB_BG_OPACITY] ?: 0.25f,
                subtitleDropShadowStrength = prefs[Keys.SUB_SHADOW_STRENGTH] ?: 0.70f,
                subtitleOutlineWidth = prefs[Keys.SUB_OUTLINE_WIDTH] ?: 0.55f,
                subtitleBold = prefs[Keys.SUB_BOLD] ?: true,
                subtitleBottomMarginDp = prefs[Keys.SUB_BOTTOM_MARGIN] ?: 28f
            )
        }

    suspend fun setThemeMode(mode: AppThemeMode) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.THEME_MODE] = mode.name }
    }

    suspend fun setAppIconColor(iconColor: AppIconColor) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.APP_ICON_COLOR] = iconColor.name }
        AppLauncherIconManager.applyLauncherIcon(context, iconColor)
    }

    suspend fun setLibraryViewMode(mode: LibraryViewMode) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.VIEW_MODE] = mode.name }
    }

    suspend fun setFolderContentViewMode(mode: FolderContentViewMode) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.FOLDER_CONTENT_VIEW_MODE] = mode.name }
    }

    suspend fun setSortOption(field: LibrarySortField, ascending: Boolean) {
        context.dataStore.edit {
            it[Keys.SORT_FIELD] = field.name
            it[Keys.SORT_ASC] = ascending
        }
    }

    suspend fun setDefaultDecoderMode(mode: DecoderMode) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.DEFAULT_DECODER] = mode.name }
    }

    suspend fun setHoldFastForwardSpeed(speed: Float) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.HOLD_FF_SPEED] = speed.coerceIn(1.25f, 3.5f) }
    }

    suspend fun setSkipIntervalSeconds(seconds: Int) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.SKIP_INTERVAL_SEC] = seconds.coerceIn(3, 30) }
    }

    suspend fun setAutoPipEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.AUTO_PIP] = enabled }
    }

    suspend fun setBackgroundAudioEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        context.dataStore.edit { it[Keys.BG_AUDIO] = enabled }
    }

    suspend fun toggleTopLeftSlot(slot: TopLeftQuickSlot) {
        context.dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            val current = (prefs[Keys.TOP_LEFT_SLOTS] ?: "SPEED,BOOKMARK,LOOP,ORIENTATION")
                .split(",")
                .map { it.trim() }
                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
                .toMutableList()
            if (slot.key in current) current.remove(slot.key) else current.add(slot.key)
            prefs[Keys.TOP_LEFT_SLOTS] = current.joinToString(",")
        }
    }

    suspend fun toggleTopRightSlot(slot: TopRightQuickSlot) {
        context.dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            val current = (prefs[Keys.TOP_RIGHT_SLOTS] ?: "LOCK,DECODER")
                .split(",")
                .map { it.trim() }
                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
                .toMutableList()
            if (slot.key in current) current.remove(slot.key) else current.add(slot.key)
            prefs[Keys.TOP_RIGHT_SLOTS] = current.joinToString(",")
        }
    }

    suspend fun updateSubtitleStyle(
        font: SubtitleFontChoice? = null,
        sizeSp: Float? = null,
        colorArgb: Int? = null,
        bgOpacity: Float? = null,
        shadowStrength: Float? = null,
        outlineWidth: Float? = null,
        bold: Boolean? = null,
        bottomMarginDp: Float? = null
    ) {
        context.dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            font?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_FONT] = it.name }
            sizeSp?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_SIZE_SP] = it.coerceIn(14f, 42f) }
            colorArgb?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_COLOR] = it }
            bgOpacity?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_BG_OPACITY] = it.coerceIn(0f, 1f) }
            shadowStrength?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_SHADOW_STRENGTH] = it.coerceIn(0f, 1f) }
            outlineWidth?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_OUTLINE_WIDTH] = it.coerceIn(0f, 1.5f) }
            bold?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_BOLD] = it }
            bottomMarginDp?.let { prefs[Keys.SUB_BOTTOM_MARGIN] = it.coerceIn(8f, 120f) }
        }
    }
}

)

fun resolveSubtitleFontFamily(choice: SubtitleFontChoice): FontFamily {
    return when (choice) {
        SubtitleFontChoice.SANS_SERIF -> FontFamily.SansSerif
        SubtitleFontChoice.SERIF -> FontFamily.Serif
        SubtitleFontChoice.PLUS_JAKARTA -> PlusJakartaFontFamily
        SubtitleFontChoice.OPEN_SANS -> OpenSansFontFamily
    }
}

val LocalVdxTokens = staticCompositionLocalOf { resolveVdxTokens(AppThemeMode.OCEAN) }

@Composable
fun FlagshipVideoPlayerTheme(
    themeMode: AppThemeMode,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val tokens = remember(themeMode) { resolveVdxTokens(themeMode) }
    val view = LocalView.current

    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = (view.context as? Activity)?.window ?: return@SideEffect
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            window.statusBarColor = tokens.paper.toArgb()
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            window.navigationBarColor = tokens.paper.toArgb()
            val controller = WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view)
            controller.isAppearanceLightStatusBars = !tokens.isDark
            controller.isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = !tokens.isDark
        }
    }

    val colorScheme = if (tokens.isDark) {
        darkColorScheme(
            primary = tokens.primary,
            onPrimary = tokens.onColor,
            primaryContainer = tokens.primary,
            onPrimaryContainer = tokens.onColor,
            secondary = tokens.secondary,
            onSecondary = tokens.onColor,
            secondaryContainer = tokens.secondary,
            onSecondaryContainer = tokens.onColor,
            tertiary = tokens.tertiary,
            onTertiary = tokens.onColor,
            background = tokens.paper,
            onBackground = tokens.ink,
            surface = tokens.card,
            onSurface = tokens.ink,
            surfaceVariant = tokens.card,
            onSurfaceVariant = tokens.muted,
            error = tokens.danger
        )
    } else {
        lightColorScheme(
            primary = tokens.primary,
            onPrimary = tokens.onColor,
            primaryContainer = tokens.primary,
            onPrimaryContainer = tokens.onColor,
            secondary = tokens.secondary,
            onSecondary = tokens.onColor,
            secondaryContainer = tokens.secondary,
            onSecondaryContainer = tokens.onColor,
            tertiary = tokens.tertiary,
            onTertiary = tokens.onColor,
            background = tokens.paper,
            onBackground = tokens.ink,
            surface = tokens.card,
            onSurface = tokens.ink,
            surfaceVariant = tokens.paper,
            onSurfaceVariant = tokens.muted,
            error = tokens.danger
        )
    }

    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalVdxTokens provides tokens) {
        MaterialTheme(
            colorScheme = colorScheme,
            typography = FlagshipTypography,
            content = content
        )
    }
}

data class VideoDynamicPalette(
    val primaryAccent: Color = Color(0xFF4D9CFF),
    val onPrimaryAccent: Color = Color(0xFF111111),
    val primaryGlow: Color = Color(0x664D9CFF),
    val tertiaryLoopAccent: Color = Color(0xFF5CE1E6),
    val osdSurface: Color = Color(0xFF0E0F12).copy(alpha = 0.84f),
    val osdElevatedSurface: Color = Color(0xFF1E1F24).copy(alpha = 0.92f),
    val specularBorder: Color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f)
)

object DynamicVideoAccentExtractor {
    fun buildFromThemeTokens(tokens: VdxThemeTokens): VideoDynamicPalette {
        return VideoDynamicPalette(
            primaryAccent = tokens.primary,
            onPrimaryAccent = tokens.onColor,
            primaryGlow = tokens.primary.copy(alpha = 0.42f),
            tertiaryLoopAccent = tokens.secondary
        )
    }
}

object FormatUtils {
    fun formatDurationMs(durationMs: Long): String {
        val safeMs = durationMs.coerceAtLeast(0L)
        val totalSec = safeMs / 1000L
        val hours = totalSec / 3600L
        val minutes = (totalSec % 3600L) / 60L
        val seconds = totalSec % 60L
        return if (hours > 0L) {
            String.format(Locale.US, "%d:%02d:%02d", hours, minutes, seconds)
        } else {
            String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d", minutes, seconds)
        }
    }

    fun formatPreciseCentiseconds(timeMs: Long): String {
        val safeMs = timeMs.coerceAtLeast(0L)
        val totalSec = safeMs / 1000L
        val hours = totalSec / 3600L
        val minutes = (totalSec % 3600L) / 60L
        val seconds = totalSec % 60L
        val centis = (safeMs % 1000L) / 10L
        return if (hours > 0L) {
            String.format(Locale.US, "%d:%02d:%02d.%02d", hours, minutes, seconds, centis)
        } else {
            String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d.%02d", minutes, seconds, centis)
        }
    }

    fun formatSignedSeconds(offsetMs: Long): String {
        val seconds = offsetMs / 1000.0
        val sign = if (offsetMs > 0) "+" else ""
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%s%.2fs", sign, seconds)
    }

    fun formatFileSize(sizeBytes: Long): String {
        if (sizeBytes <= 0L) return "0 B"
        val kb = 1024.0
        val mb = kb * 1024.0
        val gb = mb * 1024.0
        return when {
            sizeBytes >= gb -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f GB", sizeBytes / gb)
            sizeBytes >= mb -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f MB", sizeBytes / mb)
            sizeBytes >= kb -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.0f KB", sizeBytes / kb)
            else -> "$sizeBytes B"
        }
    }

    fun formatDateModified(epochSec: Long): String {
        if (epochSec <= 0L) return "Unknown"
        return runCatching {
            val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm", Locale.US)
            sdf.format(Date(epochSec * 1000L))
        }.getOrDefault("Unknown")
    }

    fun computeResolutionBadge(width: Int, height: Int): String {
        val maxDim = maxOf(width, height)
        val minDim = minOf(width, height)
        return when {
            maxDim >= 7600 || minDim >= 4300 -> "8K"
            maxDim >= 3800 || minDim >= 2100 -> "4K"
            maxDim >= 2500 || minDim >= 1400 -> "1440p"
            maxDim >= 1900 || minDim >= 1000 -> "1080p"
            maxDim >= 1200 || minDim >= 700 -> "720p"
            maxDim >= 800 || minDim >= 480 -> "480p"
            maxDim > 0 -> "${minDim}p"
            else -> "Video"
        }
    }
}

cat << 'EOF' > app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/engine/VdxBackgroundPlaybackService.kt
package com.flagship.videoplayer.engine

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.flagship.videoplayer.MainActivity

class VdxBackgroundPlaybackService : Service() {

    companion object {
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "vdx_bg_playback_channel"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 4201
        private const val ACTION_START_OR_UPDATE = "com.flagship.videoplayer.BG_UPDATE"
        private const val ACTION_TOGGLE_PLAY = "com.flagship.videoplayer.BG_TOGGLE"
        private const val ACTION_STOP = "com.flagship.videoplayer.BG_STOP"
        private const val EXTRA_TITLE = "extra_title"
        private const val EXTRA_IS_PLAYING = "extra_is_playing"

        @Volatile
        var onTogglePlayPause: (() -> Unit)? = null

        @Volatile
        var onStopPlayback: (() -> Unit)? = null

        fun startOrUpdate(context: Context, title: String, isPlaying: Boolean) {
            val intent = Intent(context, VdxBackgroundPlaybackService::class.java).apply {
                action = ACTION_START_OR_UPDATE
                putExtra(EXTRA_TITLE, title)
                putExtra(EXTRA_IS_PLAYING, isPlaying)
            }
            runCatching {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                    context.startForegroundService(intent)
                } else {
                    context.startService(intent)
                }
            }
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            runCatching {
                context.stopService(Intent(context, VdxBackgroundPlaybackService::class.java))
            }
        }
    }

    private var currentTitle: String = "VDX Player"
    private var currentlyPlaying: Boolean = true

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        ensureChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_TOGGLE_PLAY -> {
                onTogglePlayPause?.invoke()
                currentlyPlaying = !currentlyPlaying
                postForegroundNotification()
            }
            ACTION_STOP -> {
                onStopPlayback?.invoke()
                stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
                stopSelf()
            }
            else -> {
                currentTitle = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TITLE)?.ifBlank { "VDX Player" } ?: currentTitle
                currentlyPlaying = intent?.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_IS_PLAYING, true) ?: true
                postForegroundNotification()
            }
        }
        return START_NOT_STICKY
    }

    private fun ensureChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val nm = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java) ?: return
            if (nm.getNotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID) == null) {
                val channel = NotificationChannel(
                    CHANNEL_ID,
                    "Background audio playback",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
                ).apply {
                    description = "Controls for background video audio playback"
                    setShowBadge(false)
                }
                nm.createNotificationChannel(channel)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun postForegroundNotification() {
        val notif = buildNotification()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notif, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK)
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notif)
        }
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val openIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
            },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val toggleIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this,
            1,
            Intent(this, VdxBackgroundPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_TOGGLE_PLAY },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val stopIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this,
            2,
            Intent(this, VdxBackgroundPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_STOP },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val toggleIcon = if (currentlyPlaying) android.R.drawable.ic_media_pause else android.R.drawable.ic_media_play
        val toggleLabel = if (currentlyPlaying) "Pause" else "Play"

        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_media_play)
            .setContentTitle(currentTitle)
            .setContentText(if (currentlyPlaying) "Playing audio in background" else "Paused")
            .setContentIntent(openIntent)
            .setOngoing(currentlyPlaying)
            .setOnlyAlertOnce(true)
            .setVisibility(NotificationCompat.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC)
            .addAction(toggleIcon, toggleLabel, toggleIntent)
            .addAction(android.R.drawable.ic_menu_close_clear_cancel, "Stop", stopIntent)
            .build()
    }
}

cat << 'EOF' > app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/engine/VdxFloatingOverlayService.kt
package com.flagship.videoplayer.engine

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Outline
import android.graphics.PixelFormat
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import android.util.TypedValue
import android.view.GestureDetector
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.ScaleGestureDetector
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewConfiguration
import android.view.ViewOutlineProvider
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.ImageButton
import android.widget.ImageView
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.ExoPlayer
import androidx.media3.ui.AspectRatioFrameLayout
import androidx.media3.ui.PlayerView
import com.flagship.videoplayer.MainActivity
import com.flagship.videoplayer.R
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.hypot
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

class VdxFloatingOverlayService : Service() {

    companion object {
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "vdx_floating_overlay_channel"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 4202
        private const val EXTRA_TITLE = "extra_title"
        private const val EXTRA_VIDEO_W = "extra_video_w"
        private const val EXTRA_VIDEO_H = "extra_video_h"

        private val _isShowing = MutableStateFlow(false)
        val isShowing: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isShowing.asStateFlow()

        @Volatile var activePlayer: ExoPlayer? = null
        @Volatile var isPlayingFlow: StateFlow<Boolean>? = null
        @Volatile var activeSubtitleFlow: StateFlow<String?>? = null
        @Volatile var skipIntervalSecondsProvider: (() -> Int)? = null
        @Volatile var onTogglePlayPause: (() -> Unit)? = null
        @Volatile var onSeekRelativeMs: ((Long) -> Unit)? = null
        @Volatile var onReturnFullscreen: (() -> Unit)? = null
        @Volatile var onClosePlayer: (() -> Unit)? = null

        fun start(context: Context, title: String, videoWidth: Int, videoHeight: Int) {
            val intent = Intent(context, VdxFloatingOverlayService::class.java).apply {
                putExtra(EXTRA_TITLE, title)
                putExtra(EXTRA_VIDEO_W, videoWidth)
                putExtra(EXTRA_VIDEO_H, videoHeight)
            }
            runCatching {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                    context.startForegroundService(intent)
                } else {
                    context.startService(intent)
                }
            }
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            _isShowing.value = false
            runCatching {
                context.stopService(Intent(context, VdxFloatingOverlayService::class.java))
            }
        }
    }

    private val serviceScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Main.immediate)
    private lateinit var windowManager: WindowManager
    private var rootView: FrameLayout? = null
    private var playerView: PlayerView? = null
    private var controlsOverlay: FrameLayout? = null
    private var playPauseBtn: ImageButton? = null
    private var seekBadgeView: TextView? = null
    private var subtitleTextView: TextView? = null
    private var layoutParams: WindowManager.LayoutParams? = null

    private var autoHideControlsJob: Job? = null
    private var hideSeekBadgeJob: Job? = null
    private var accumulatedSeekSec: Int = 0
    private var lastSeekSide: Int = 0
    private var videoAspectRatio: Float = 16f / 9f

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        windowManager = getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
        ensureChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        val title = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TITLE)?.ifBlank { "VDX Player" } ?: "VDX Player"
        val vw = intent?.getIntExtra(EXTRA_VIDEO_W, 16) ?: 16
        val vh = intent?.getIntExtra(EXTRA_VIDEO_H, 9) ?: 9
        if (vw > 0 && vh > 0) {
            videoAspectRatio = (vw.toFloat() / vh.toFloat()).coerceIn(0.65f, 2.25f)
        }

        postForegroundNotification(title)

        if (activePlayer == null) {
            stopSelf()
            return START_NOT_STICKY
        }

        if (rootView == null) {
            createAndAttachOverlay()
        } else {
            playerView?.player = activePlayer
        }
        _isShowing.value = true
        return START_NOT_STICKY
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        _isShowing.value = false
        serviceScope.cancel()
        playerView?.player = null
        rootView?.let { view ->
            runCatching { windowManager.removeView(view) }
        }
        rootView = null
        playerView = null
        controlsOverlay = null
        playPauseBtn = null
        seekBadgeView = null
        subtitleTextView = null
    }

    private fun dp(value: Float): Int =
        TypedValue.applyDimension(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_DIP, value, resources.displayMetrics).roundToInt()

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    private fun createAndAttachOverlay() {
        val dm = resources.displayMetrics
        val initW = dp(248f).coerceAtMost((dm.widthPixels * 0.78f).roundToInt())
        val initH = (initW / videoAspectRatio).roundToInt().coerceIn(dp(120f), (dm.heightPixels * 0.48f).roundToInt())

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            initW,
            initH,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_HARDWARE_ACCELERATED,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            x = (dm.widthPixels - initW - dp(16f)).coerceAtLeast(0)
            y = dp(96f)
        }
        layoutParams = params

        val cornerRadiusPx = dp(14f).toFloat()
        val root = FrameLayout(this).apply {
            setBackgroundColor(Color.BLACK)
            outlineProvider = object : ViewOutlineProvider() {
                override fun getOutline(view: View, outline: Outline) {
                    outline.setRoundRect(0, 0, view.width, view.height, cornerRadiusPx)
                }
            }
            clipToOutline = true
            elevation = dp(10f).toFloat()
        }
        rootView = root

        // 1. Video surface (TextureView renders smoothly while dragging & resizing in WindowManager)
        val pv = (LayoutInflater.from(this).inflate(R.layout.vdx_player_texture_view, root, false) as PlayerView).apply {
            useController = false
            isClickable = false
            isFocusable = false
            subtitleView?.visibility = View.GONE
            resizeMode = AspectRatioFrameLayout.RESIZE_MODE_FIT
            player = activePlayer
        }
        playerView = pv
        root.addView(pv, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

        // 2. Live subtitle bar inside the floating window
        val subTv = TextView(this).apply {
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 10f)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            maxLines = 2
            setPadding(dp(6f), dp(2f), dp(6f), dp(2f))
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.argb(145, 0, 0, 0))
                cornerRadius = dp(5f).toFloat()
            }
            visibility = View.GONE
        }
        subtitleTextView = subTv
        val subParams = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            Gravity.BOTTOM or Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
        ).apply {
            bottomMargin = dp(6f)
            leftMargin = dp(8f)
            rightMargin = dp(8f)
        }
        root.addView(subTv, subParams)

        // 3. Double-tap seek feedback pill (-10s / +10s)
        val badge = TextView(this).apply {
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 13f)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            setPadding(dp(12f), dp(5f), dp(12f), dp(5f))
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.argb(190, 0, 0, 0))
                cornerRadius = dp(12f).toFloat()
            }
            visibility = View.GONE
        }
        seekBadgeView = badge
        root.addView(
            badge,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
                FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
                Gravity.CENTER
            )
        )

        // 4. Minimalist Controls Overlay — ONLY 3 buttons: Fullscreen, Close (X), Play/Pause
        val overlay = FrameLayout(this).apply {
            setBackgroundColor(Color.argb(110, 0, 0, 0))
            visibility = View.GONE
        }
        controlsOverlay = overlay

        fun makeCircleButton(iconRes: Int, sizeDp: Float, contentDesc: String, onClick: () -> Unit): ImageButton {
            return ImageButton(this).apply {
                setImageResource(iconRes)
                scaleType = ImageView.ScaleType.CENTER_INSIDE
                contentDescription = contentDesc
                val pad = dp(if (sizeDp > 40f) 10f else 7f)
                setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
                background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                    shape = GradientDrawable.OVAL
                    setColor(Color.argb(160, 0, 0, 0))
                    setStroke(dp(1f), Color.argb(70, 255, 255, 255))
                }
                setOnClickListener { onClick() }
            }
        }

        // Button 1: Fullscreen (Return to main player inside the app)
        val fullscreenBtn = makeCircleButton(
            iconRes = R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_fullscreen,
            sizeDp = 34f,
            contentDesc = "Return to fullscreen"
        ) {
            onReturnFullscreen?.invoke()
            stopSelf()
        }
        overlay.addView(
            fullscreenBtn,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dp(34f), dp(34f), Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START).apply {
                leftMargin = dp(8f)
                topMargin = dp(8f)
            }
        )

        // Button 2: Exit / Close (X)
        val closeBtn = makeCircleButton(
            iconRes = R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_close,
            sizeDp = 34f,
            contentDesc = "Close floating player"
        ) {
            onClosePlayer?.invoke()
            stopSelf()
        }
        overlay.addView(
            closeBtn,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dp(34f), dp(34f), Gravity.TOP or Gravity.END).apply {
                rightMargin = dp(8f)
                topMargin = dp(8f)
            }
        )

        // Button 3: Play / Pause
        val initialPlayIcon = if (isPlayingFlow?.value == true) R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_pause else R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_play
        val toggleBtn = makeCircleButton(
            iconRes = initialPlayIcon,
            sizeDp = 46f,
            contentDesc = "Play or pause"
        ) {
            onTogglePlayPause?.invoke()
            scheduleAutoHideControls()
        }
        playPauseBtn = toggleBtn
        overlay.addView(
            toggleBtn,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dp(46f), dp(46f), Gravity.CENTER)
        )

        root.addView(overlay, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

        // Collect play state & subtitles
        serviceScope.launch {
            isPlayingFlow?.collect { playing ->
                playPauseBtn?.setImageResource(
                    if (playing) R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_pause else R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_play
                )
            }
        }
        serviceScope.launch {
            activeSubtitleFlow?.collect { text ->
                val tv = subtitleTextView ?: return@collect
                if (text.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    tv.visibility = View.GONE
                } else {
                    tv.text = text
                    tv.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                }
            }
        }

        // Gestures: Single tap = toggle 3 buttons, Double tap left/right = seek by configured skip seconds, Drag = move, Pinch = resize
        val touchSlop = ViewConfiguration.get(this).scaledTouchSlop
        val gestureDetector = GestureDetector(this, object : GestureDetector.SimpleOnGestureListener() {
            override fun onDown(e: MotionEvent): Boolean = true

            override fun onSingleTapConfirmed(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                val ctrl = controlsOverlay ?: return true
                if (ctrl.visibility == View.VISIBLE) {
                    ctrl.visibility = View.GONE
                    autoHideControlsJob?.cancel()
                } else {
                    ctrl.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    scheduleAutoHideControls()
                }
                return true
            }

            override fun onDoubleTap(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                val w = root.width.coerceAtLeast(1)
                val isForward = e.x >= w * 0.5f
                val skipSec = (skipIntervalSecondsProvider?.invoke() ?: 10).coerceIn(3, 30)
                val side = if (isForward) 1 else -1
                if (side != lastSeekSide) {
                    accumulatedSeekSec = 0
                    lastSeekSide = side
                }
                accumulatedSeekSec += if (isForward) skipSec else -skipSec
                onSeekRelativeMs?.invoke((if (isForward) skipSec else -skipSec) * 1000L)
                showSeekFeedbackBadge(isForward, abs(accumulatedSeekSec))
                return true
            }
        })

        val scaleDetector = ScaleGestureDetector(this, object : ScaleGestureDetector.SimpleOnScaleGestureListener() {
            override fun onScale(detector: ScaleGestureDetector): Boolean {
                val lp = layoutParams ?: return false
                val minW = dp(170f)
                val maxW = (resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels - dp(16f)).coerceAtLeast(minW)
                val newW = (lp.width * detector.scaleFactor).roundToInt().coerceIn(minW, maxW)
                val newH = (newW / videoAspectRatio).roundToInt().coerceIn(dp(100f), (resources.displayMetrics.heightPixels * 0.65f).roundToInt())
                lp.width = newW
                lp.height = newH
                lp.x = lp.x.coerceIn(0, (resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels - newW).coerceAtLeast(0))
                lp.y = lp.y.coerceIn(0, (resources.displayMetrics.heightPixels - newH).coerceAtLeast(0))
                runCatching { windowManager.updateViewLayout(root, lp) }
                return true
            }
        })

        var downRawX = 0f
        var downRawY = 0f
        var startParamX = 0
        var startParamY = 0
        var isDragging = false

        root.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            scaleDetector.onTouchEvent(event)
            if (event.pointerCount > 1 || scaleDetector.isInProgress) {
                isDragging = false
                return@setOnTouchListener true
            }
            gestureDetector.onTouchEvent(event)

            when (event.actionMasked) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    downRawX = event.rawX
                    downRawY = event.rawY
                    startParamX = params.x
                    startParamY = params.y
                    isDragging = false
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    val dx = event.rawX - downRawX
                    val dy = event.rawY - downRawY
                    if (!isDragging && hypot(dx.toDouble(), dy.toDouble()) > touchSlop) {
                        isDragging = true
                    }
                    if (isDragging) {
                        val maxX = (resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels - params.width).coerceAtLeast(0)
                        val maxY = (resources.displayMetrics.heightPixels - params.height).coerceAtLeast(0)
                        params.x = (startParamX + dx.roundToInt()).coerceIn(0, maxX)
                        params.y = (startParamY + dy.roundToInt()).coerceIn(0, maxY)
                        runCatching { windowManager.updateViewLayout(root, params) }
                    }
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    isDragging = false
                }
            }
            true
        }

        windowManager.addView(root, params)
    }

    private fun scheduleAutoHideControls() {
        autoHideControlsJob?.cancel()
        autoHideControlsJob = serviceScope.launch {
            delay(3000L)
            controlsOverlay?.visibility = View.GONE
        }
    }

    private fun showSeekFeedbackBadge(isForward: Boolean, totalAbsSec: Int) {
        val badge = seekBadgeView ?: return
        badge.text = if (isForward) "+${totalAbsSec}s" else "-${totalAbsSec}s"
        val lp = badge.layoutParams as? FrameLayout.LayoutParams
        if (lp != null) {
            lp.gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL or (if (isForward) Gravity.END else Gravity.START)
            lp.leftMargin = if (isForward) 0 else dp(14f)
            lp.rightMargin = if (isForward) dp(14f) else 0
            badge.layoutParams = lp
        }
        badge.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        hideSeekBadgeJob?.cancel()
        hideSeekBadgeJob = serviceScope.launch {
            delay(650L)
            badge.visibility = View.GONE
            accumulatedSeekSec = 0
            lastSeekSide = 0
        }
    }

    private fun ensureChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val nm = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java) ?: return
            if (nm.getNotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID) == null) {
                val channel = NotificationChannel(
                    CHANNEL_ID,
                    "Floating video player",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
                ).apply {
                    description = "Active while floating pop-up video player is open"
                    setShowBadge(false)
                }
                nm.createNotificationChannel(channel)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun postForegroundNotification(title: String) {
        val openIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            10,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_REORDER_TO_FRONT
            },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val notif: Notification = NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_media_play)
            .setContentTitle(title)
            .setContentText("Floating player active • Tap to return fullscreen")
            .setContentIntent(openIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setOnlyAlertOnce(true)
            .build()

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notif, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK)
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notif)
        }
    }
}
