                            Text(
                                "${FormatUtils.formatPreciseCentiseconds(loop.startMs)} - ${FormatUtils.formatPreciseCentiseconds(loop.endMs)}",
                                style = TabularMonoStyle,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = palette.tertiaryLoopAccent
                            )
                        }
                        IconButton(onClick = { editingLoop = loop }, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)) {
                            Icon(Icons.Default.Edit, contentDescription = "Rename loop", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                        }
                        IconButton(onClick = { onDeleteSavedLoop(loop) }, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)) {
                            Icon(Icons.Default.DeleteOutline, contentDescription = "Delete loop", tint = Color(0xFFFF5C5C), modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        HorizontalDivider(color = palette.specularBorder)

        Button(
            onClick = { onAddBookmarkNow("") },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            Icon(Icons.Default.BookmarkAdd, contentDescription = null)
            Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
            Text("Pin bookmark at current frame", color = palette.onPrimaryAccent)
        }

        Text(
            "Bookmarks (${bookmarks.size})",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
            color = palette.primaryAccent
        )
        bookmarks.forEach { bm ->
            Surface(
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.07f),
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, palette.specularBorder),
                onClick = { onSeekToExact(bm.timestampMs) },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(10.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(bm.label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                        Text(
                            FormatUtils.formatPreciseCentiseconds(bm.timestampMs),
                            style = TabularMonoStyle,
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            color = palette.tertiaryLoopAccent
                        )
                    }
                    IconButton(onClick = { editingBookmark = bm }, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.Edit, contentDescription = "Rename", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                    }
                    IconButton(onClick = { onDeleteBookmark(bm) }, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.DeleteOutline, contentDescription = "Delete", tint = Color(0xFFFF5C5C), modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    editingLoop?.let { target ->
        var labelText by remember(target) { mutableStateOf(target.label) }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { editingLoop = null },
            title = { Text("Rename saved loop") },
            text = {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = labelText,
                    onValueChange = { labelText = it },
                    singleLine = true,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                Button(onClick = {
                    onRenameSavedLoop(target, labelText)
                    editingLoop = null
                }) { Text("Save") }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { editingLoop = null }) { Text("Cancel") }
            }
        )
    }

    editingBookmark?.let { target ->
        var labelText by remember(target) { mutableStateOf(target.label) }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { editingBookmark = null },
            title = { Text("Edit bookmark") },
            text = {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = labelText,
                    onValueChange = { labelText = it },
                    singleLine = true,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                Button(onClick = {
                    onUpdateBookmarkLabel(target, labelText)
                    editingBookmark = null
                }) { Text("Save") }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { editingBookmark = null }) { Text("Cancel") }
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SpeedSideSheetContent(
    palette: VideoDynamicPalette,
    playbackSpeed: Float,
    onSetPlaybackSpeed: (Float) -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        Surface(
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f),
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, palette.specularBorder),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    HoldRepeatStepButton(
                        text = "-0.05x",
                        modifier = Modifier.width(68.dp),
                        onStep = { onSetPlaybackSpeed((playbackSpeed - 0.05f).coerceAtLeast(0.25f)) }
                    )

                    Text(
                        text = String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", playbackSpeed),
                        style = TabularMonoStyle,
                        fontSize = 24.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = palette.primaryAccent
                    )

                    HoldRepeatStepButton(
                        text = "+0.05x",
                        modifier = Modifier.width(68.dp),
                        onStep = { onSetPlaybackSpeed((playbackSpeed + 0.05f).coerceAtMost(3.5f)) }
                    )
                }

                Slider(
                    value = playbackSpeed,
                    onValueChange = { onSetPlaybackSpeed((it * 20f).roundToInt() / 20f) },
                    valueRange = 0.25f..3.0f
                )
            }
        }

        val essentialPresets = listOf(0.75f, 1.0f, 1.25f, 1.5f, 2.0f)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            essentialPresets.forEach { spd ->
                val selected = abs(playbackSpeed - spd) < 0.02f
                val label = when (spd) {
                    1.0f -> "1x"
                    1.5f -> "1.5x"
                    2.0f -> "2x"
                    else -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", spd)
                }
                Surface(
                    shape = CircleShape,
                    color = if (selected) palette.primaryAccent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.07f),
                    border = BorderStroke(
                        1.dp,
                        if (selected) palette.primaryAccent else palette.specularBorder
                    ),
                    onClick = { onSetPlaybackSpeed(spd) },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(38.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Text(
                            text = label,
                            style = TabularMonoStyle,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = if (selected) palette.onPrimaryAccent else Color.White,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CustomizeHudControlsContent(
    palette: VideoDynamicPalette,
    enabledTopLeftSlots: List<TopLeftQuickSlot>,
    enabledTopRightSlots: List<TopRightQuickSlot>,
    onToggleTopLeftSlot: (TopLeftQuickSlot) -> Unit,
    onToggleTopRightSlot: (TopRightQuickSlot) -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "Any button hidden from the top bar automatically moves into the top-right Menu.",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
            color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.75f)
        )

        Text(
            text = "Top-left buttons (Under video title)",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
            color = palette.primaryAccent
        )
        TopLeftQuickSlot.entries.forEach { slot ->
            val isSelected = slot in enabledTopLeftSlots
            TrackRowItem(
                title = slot.label,
                badge = if (isSelected) "Top-left" else "In menu",
                selected = isSelected,
                palette = palette,
                onClick = { onToggleTopLeftSlot(slot) }
            )
        }

        HorizontalDivider(color = palette.specularBorder)

        Text(
            text = "Top-right buttons (Beside menu)",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
            color = palette.primaryAccent
        )
        TopRightQuickSlot.entries.forEach { slot ->
            val isSelected = slot in enabledTopRightSlots
            TrackRowItem(
                title = slot.label,
                badge = if (isSelected) "Top-right" else "In menu",
                selected = isSelected,
                palette = palette,
                onClick = { onToggleTopRightSlot(slot) }
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun TrackRowItem(
    title: String,
    badge: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    palette: VideoDynamicPalette,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Surface(
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
        color = if (selected) palette.primaryAccent.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.06f),
        border = BorderStroke(
            1.dp,
            if (selected) palette.primaryAccent else palette.specularBorder
        ),
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 9.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                color = Color.White,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
            Text(
                text = badge,
                style = TabularMonoStyle,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = if (selected) palette.primaryAccent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.7f)
            )
        }
    }
}

cat << 'EOF' > app/src/main/java/com/flagship/videoplayer/MainActivity.kt
package com.flagship.videoplayer

import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.PictureInPictureParams
import android.app.RemoteAction
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.graphics.drawable.Icon
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import android.provider.Settings
import android.util.Rational
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import com.flagship.videoplayer.data.*
import com.flagship.videoplayer.engine.PlayerViewModel
import com.flagship.videoplayer.engine.VdxBackgroundPlaybackService
import com.flagship.videoplayer.engine.VdxFloatingOverlayService
import com.flagship.videoplayer.ui.home.HomeLibraryScreen
import com.flagship.videoplayer.ui.home.HomeLibraryViewModel
import com.flagship.videoplayer.ui.player.FlagshipPlayerScreen
import com.flagship.videoplayer.ui.theme.FlagshipVideoPlayerTheme
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    companion object {
        private const val ACTION_PIP_TOGGLE = "com.flagship.videoplayer.PIP_TOGGLE"
    }

    private lateinit var homeViewModel: HomeLibraryViewModel
    private lateinit var playerViewModel: PlayerViewModel
    private var isInPipModeState by mutableStateOf(false)
    private var isActivityForeground = false

    private val pipActionReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
            if (intent?.action == ACTION_PIP_TOGGLE) {
                playerViewModel.togglePlayPause()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()

        val database = FlagshipDatabase.getInstance(applicationContext)
        val prefsRepo = AppPreferencesRepository(applicationContext)

        homeViewModel = ViewModelProvider(
            this,
            HomeLibraryViewModel.Factory(applicationContext, database.dao(), prefsRepo)
        )[HomeLibraryViewModel::class.java]

        playerViewModel = ViewModelProvider(
            this,
            PlayerViewModel.Factory(applicationContext, database.dao(), prefsRepo)
        )[PlayerViewModel::class.java]

        ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
            this,
            pipActionReceiver,
            IntentFilter(ACTION_PIP_TOGGLE),
            ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED
        )

        lifecycleScope.launch {
            combine(
                playerViewModel.activeVideo,
                playerViewModel.isPlaying,
                playerViewModel.preferencesState
            ) { video, playing, prefs ->
                Triple(video, playing, prefs)
            }.collect { (video, playing, prefs) ->
                val canOverlay = Settings.canDrawOverlays(this@MainActivity)
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O && video != null) {
                    runCatching {
                        setPictureInPictureParams(
                            buildFallbackSystemPipParams(
                                video = video,
                                isPlaying = playing,
                                autoEnter = prefs.autoPipEnabled && playing && !canOverlay
                            )
                        )
                    }
                } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                    runCatching {
                        setPictureInPictureParams(
                            PictureInPictureParams.Builder()
                                .setAutoEnterEnabled(false)
                                .build()
                        )
                    }
                }

                if (!isActivityForeground &&
                    !isInPipModeState &&
                    !VdxFloatingOverlayService.isShowing.value &&
                    prefs.backgroundAudioEnabled
                ) {
                    if (video != null) {
                        VdxBackgroundPlaybackService.startOrUpdate(
                            this@MainActivity,
                            video.titleWithoutExt,
                            playing
                        )
                    } else {
                        VdxBackgroundPlaybackService.stop(this@MainActivity)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        handleIncomingViewIntent(intent)

        setContent {
            val prefs by homeViewModel.preferencesState.collectAsState()
            val activeVideo by playerViewModel.activeVideo.collectAsState()

            FlagshipVideoPlayerTheme(
                themeMode = prefs.themeMode
            ) {
                if (activeVideo != null) {
                    FlagshipPlayerScreen(
                        viewModel = playerViewModel,
                        isInPipMode = isInPipModeState,
                        onEnterPipMode = { launchFloatingPlayerOrAskPermission() },
                        onExitPlayer = {
                            VdxFloatingOverlayService.stop(this@MainActivity)
                            playerViewModel.stopAndReleasePlayer()
                            homeViewModel.refreshLibrarySilently()
                        }
                    )
                } else {
                    HomeLibraryScreen(
                        viewModel = homeViewModel,
                        onPlayVideo = { videoItem, playlist, autoResume ->
                            playerViewModel.loadAndPlayVideo(videoItem, playlist, autoResume)
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        runCatching { unregisterReceiver(pipActionReceiver) }
        if (isFinishing) {
            VdxFloatingOverlayService.stop(this)
        }
    }

    override fun onStart() {
        super.onStart()
        isActivityForeground = true
        VdxBackgroundPlaybackService.stop(this)
        if (VdxFloatingOverlayService.isShowing.value) {
            VdxFloatingOverlayService.stop(this)
        }
    }

    override fun onStop() {
        super.onStop()
        isActivityForeground = false
        val activeVid = playerViewModel.activeVideo.value
        val keepBgAudio = playerViewModel.preferencesState.value.backgroundAudioEnabled
        if (!isInPipModeState && !VdxFloatingOverlayService.isShowing.value && activeVid != null) {
            if (keepBgAudio && playerViewModel.isPlaying.value) {
                VdxBackgroundPlaybackService.startOrUpdate(
                    this,
                    activeVid.titleWithoutExt,
                    true
                )
            } else if (!keepBgAudio) {
                playerViewModel.exoPlayerFlow.value?.pause()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onNewIntent(intent: Intent) {
        super.onNewIntent(intent)
        handleIncomingViewIntent(intent)
    }

    private fun handleIncomingViewIntent(intent: Intent?) {
        val dataUri: Uri = intent?.data ?: return
        val scheme = dataUri.scheme?.lowercase() ?: ""
        val isNetwork = scheme == "http" || scheme == "https" || scheme == "rtsp"

        var resolvedName = dataUri.lastPathSegment?.substringAfterLast('/') ?: "External media"
        var resolvedSize = 0L
        if (!isNetwork && scheme == "content") {
            runCatching {
                contentResolver.query(dataUri, null, null, null, null)?.use { cursor ->
                    if (cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                        val nameIdx = cursor.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)
                        if (nameIdx >= 0) {
                            cursor.getString(nameIdx)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let { resolvedName = it }
                        }
                        val sizeIdx = cursor.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.SIZE)
                        if (sizeIdx >= 0) {
                            resolvedSize = cursor.getLong(sizeIdx).coerceAtLeast(0L)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        val fingerprint = if (isNetwork) {
            FileFingerprintEngine.computeStreamFingerprint(dataUri.toString())
        } else {
            FileFingerprintEngine.computeQuickFingerprint(resolvedName, resolvedSize)
        }

        val externalItem = LocalVideoItem(
            mediaStoreId = -1L,
            contentUri = dataUri,
            filePath = dataUri.toString(),
            folderPath = "external://intent",
            folderName = "External videos",
            displayName = resolvedName,
            titleWithoutExt = resolvedName.substringBeforeLast('.'),
            extensionBadge = if (isNetwork) NetworkStreamHistoryEntity.detectProtocolBadge(dataUri.toString()) else "VIDEO",
            sizeBytes = resolvedSize,
            durationMs = 0L,
            width = 1920,
            height = 1080,
            resolutionBadge = "HD",
            dateModifiedSec = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000L,
            sidecarSubtitlePaths = emptyList(),
            fileFingerprint = fingerprint,
            isNetworkStream = isNetwork
        )
        playerViewModel.loadAndPlayVideo(externalItem, listOf(externalItem), autoResume = true)
    }

    private fun startCustomFloatingOverlay(moveAppToBack: Boolean) {
        val video = playerViewModel.activeVideo.value ?: return
        val player = playerViewModel.exoPlayerFlow.value ?: return

        VdxFloatingOverlayService.activePlayer = player
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.isPlayingFlow = playerViewModel.isPlaying
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.activeSubtitleFlow = playerViewModel.activeSubtitleLines
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.skipIntervalSecondsProvider = {
            playerViewModel.preferencesState.value.skipIntervalSeconds
        }
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.onTogglePlayPause = {
            playerViewModel.togglePlayPause()
        }
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.onSeekRelativeMs = { deltaMs ->
            playerViewModel.seekRelativeMs(deltaMs)
        }
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.onReturnFullscreen = {
            val returnIntent = Intent(applicationContext, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or
                    Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or
                    Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_REORDER_TO_FRONT
            }
            applicationContext.startActivity(returnIntent)
        }
        VdxFloatingOverlayService.onClosePlayer = {
            playerViewModel.stopAndReleasePlayer()
        }

        VdxFloatingOverlayService.start(
            context = this,
            title = video.titleWithoutExt,
            videoWidth = video.width,
            videoHeight = video.height
        )
        if (moveAppToBack) {
            moveTaskToBack(true)
        }
    }

    private fun launchFloatingPlayerOrAskPermission() {
        if (Settings.canDrawOverlays(this)) {
            startCustomFloatingOverlay(moveAppToBack = true)
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(
                this,
                "Allow 'Display over other apps' once for double-tap floating player",
                Toast.LENGTH_LONG
            ).show()
            runCatching {
                startActivity(
                    Intent(
                        Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION,
                        Uri.parse("package:$packageName")
                    )
                )
            }
        }
    }

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
    private fun buildFallbackSystemPipParams(
        video: LocalVideoItem?,
        isPlaying: Boolean,
        autoEnter: Boolean
    ): PictureInPictureParams {
        val w = (video?.width ?: 16).coerceIn(1, 3840)
        val h = (video?.height ?: 9).coerceIn(1, 2160)
        val rational = Rational(w, h).let {
            if (it.toFloat() in 0.42f..2.38f) it else Rational(16, 9)
        }

        val toggleAction = RemoteAction(
            Icon.createWithResource(
                this,
                if (isPlaying) R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_pause else R.drawable.ic_vdx_pip_play
            ),
            if (isPlaying) "Pause" else "Play",
            if (isPlaying) "Pause playback" else "Resume playback",
            PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                this,
                102,
                Intent(ACTION_PIP_TOGGLE).setPackage(packageName),
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
            )
        )

        val builder = PictureInPictureParams.Builder()
            .setAspectRatio(rational)
            .setActions(listOf(toggleAction))

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            builder.setSeamlessResizeEnabled(false)
            builder.setAutoEnterEnabled(autoEnter)
        }
        return builder.build()
    }

    override fun onUserLeaveHint() {
        super.onUserLeaveHint()
        val isPlaying = playerViewModel.isPlaying.value
        val autoPip = playerViewModel.preferencesState.value.autoPipEnabled
        if (playerViewModel.activeVideo.value != null && isPlaying && autoPip && !isInPipModeState) {
            if (Settings.canDrawOverlays(this)) {
                startCustomFloatingOverlay(moveAppToBack = false)
            } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT in Build.VERSION_CODES.O until Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                runCatching {
                    enterPictureInPictureMode(
                        buildFallbackSystemPipParams(
                            video = playerViewModel.activeVideo.value,
                            isPlaying = true,
                            autoEnter = false
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onPictureInPictureModeChanged(
        isInPictureInPictureMode: Boolean,
        newConfig: Configuration
    ) {
        super.onPictureInPictureModeChanged(isInPictureInPictureMode, newConfig)
        isInPipModeState = isInPictureInPictureMode
        if (!isInPictureInPictureMode && !lifecycle.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.STARTED)) {
            playerViewModel.stopAndReleasePlayer()
        }
    }
}
